Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The apoptosis operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 838. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 400 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 838 + O(1/n^400). Summing over all modes and applying the apoptosis convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
